(ordinary space, time, extra times), in units of \(H\): Einstein
\((-10, -12, -10)\), which is Israel's
\(-([K^a{}_b] - \delta^a{}_b[K])\) with the jump \([K]\) of the
extrinsic curvature FD-5, Gauss-Bonnet \((4, 8, 4)\), cubic
\((-2/5, -12/5, -2/5)\)). This is the arrangement of Randall and
Sundrum: a vacuum bulk with a negative cosmological constant and a fold
of pure tension (FD-8). The late universe, whose expansion rate is tiny
in units of \(H\), is the static block plus the expansion of FE-33. With
the author's original weights the static block needed the stabiliser in
the bulk and the source \((-61/8, -31/4, -61/8)H/\kappa\) at the fold, a
tension plus a pressureless part. Status: DERIVED
(\texttt{um3\_fold.py}, J5; \texttt{um3\_stabiliser.py}, ST9).

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\mathsf{U} = \mathsf{A},\quad \mathsf{U}_1 = \mathsf{A}_1\ \ \big(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4 = \text{constant},\ \mathcal{Z} = 1\big):\qquad
 \kappa\,\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{bulk}}{}^a{}_b = 0\ \ \text{for any } a_4(x_4),\\
&\frac{\kappa\,\mathcal{S}^{(i)}{}_{(i)}}{H} = -\frac{16}{15}\big(6 + 3\mathsf{A}^2 + 2\mathsf{A}_1\big),\qquad
 \frac{\kappa\,\mathcal{S}^{(4)}{}_{(4)}}{H} = -\frac{16}{5}\big(2 + \mathsf{A}^2\big),\qquad
 \frac{\kappa\,\mathcal{S}^{(r)}{}_{(r)}}{H} = -\frac45\big(8 + 8\mathsf{A}^2 + \mathsf{A}^4 + 4\mathsf{A}_1 + \mathsf{A}^2\mathsf{A}_1\big),\\
&3a_4'^2 = -\kappa_7\Big(\mathcal{S}^{(4)}{}_{(4)} + \frac{32H}{5\kappa}\Big),\qquad
 -\big(2a_4'' + 3a_4'^2\big) = \kappa_7\Big(\mathcal{S}^{(i)}{}_{(i)} + \frac{32H}{5\kappa}\Big),\qquad \kappa_7 = \frac{15}{16}\,\kappa H
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-33}
\] \textbf{Symbols in (FE-33).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathsf{U}\): The rate of change of \(b_4\) in units of \(H\),
  \(\mathsf{U} = b_4'/(Hb_4)\). Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(\mathsf{U} = \mathsf{A}\) with frozen extra times.
\item
  \(\mathsf{A}\): The expansion rate of ordinary space in units of
  \(H\), \(\mathsf{A} = a_4'/H\). Unit: dimensionless. Value: 0 at the
  static block; \(H_4^{(0)}/H\) today (\(H_4^{(0)}\) the Hubble
  constant); 1 at the author's vacuum, which is not a solution with the
  corrected weights.
\item
  \(\mathsf{U}_1\): The time derivative of \(\mathsf{U}\) in units of
  \(H\), \(\mathsf{U}_1 = \mathsf{U}'/H\). Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathsf{A}_1\): The time derivative of \(\mathsf{A}\) in units of
  \(H\), \(\mathsf{A}_1 = \mathsf{A}'/H = a_4''/H^2\). Unit:
  dimensionless.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(a_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) that scales
  ordinary 3-space: on the fold \(\mathrm{e}^{a_4}\) is the scale factor
  of cosmology and \(a_4'\) the Hubble rate. Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(b_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) in the
  extra-time components, \(b_4 > 0\), never identically 1 (the author's
  order of 2026-10-24); the scale factor (the factor of the lengths) of
  the extra times is \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4\) on the fold and
  \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4\,\mathrm{e}^{-H\lvert\sigma\rvert}\) at the
  distance \(\sigma\) from it (the square root of the warp factor times
  \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{Z}\): The fold factor,
  \(\mathcal{Z}(x_4) = V_{\mathrm{t}}(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4)^3/V_*\): the
  factor by which the size of the extra times multiplies every term of
  the fold; \(\mathcal{Z} = 1\) when the extra times are frozen at their
  late value. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 1 in the late universe.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\kappa\): The eight-dimensional gravitational coupling,
  \(\kappa = 8\pi G_8\). Unit: GeV\(^{-6}\). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{bulk}}\): The energy-momentum tensor of all
  sources of the bulk (with the corrected weights dirac16complex alone,
  \(\mathcal{T}_\Psi\); with the author's original weights also the
  stabiliser); \(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{bulk}}{}^{(0)}{}_b\) is its flux
  along \(\sigma\) (frame components). Unit: GeV\(^8\). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(a\): A frame (orthonormal) index running over \(0, 1, \dots, 7\)
  (frame direction \((0)\) along \(\sigma\), \((1), (2), (3)\) along
  ordinary space, \((4)\) along the time, \((5), (6), (7)\) along the
  extra times); raised and lowered with the frame metric
  \(\eta_{ab} = \mathrm{diag}(1, 1, 1, 1, -1, -1, -1, -1)\); on the fold
  (\(a, b \in \{1, \dots, 7\}\)) the frame of the fold; a repeated index
  is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(b\): A frame index (as \(a\)). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; a prime on a function of \(x_4\) alone is
  \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit: length (= time, \(c = 1\)).
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{S}^a{}_b\): The thin source of the fold: the
  energy-momentum tensor concentrated on the fold, integrated across it,
  \(\kappa\mathcal{S}^a{}_b = \int_{0^-}^{0^+}\mathrm{d}\sigma\sum_p\alpha_{(p)}\mathcal{E}_{(p)}{}^a{}_b\)
  (FD-11, the junction conditions); \(\mathcal{S}^4{}_4\) is minus its
  energy density, \(\mathcal{S}^1{}_1\) and \(\mathcal{S}^5{}_5\) its
  pressures along ordinary space and along the extra times (frame
  components along the fold, eight-dimensional normalisation: an energy
  per unit volume of the six other directions of the fold). Unit:
  GeV\(^7\). Value:
  \(\kappa\mathcal{S}^a{}_b = -\frac{32}{5}H\delta^a{}_b\) at the static
  block. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(i\): A generation index (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)), which also labels the
  neutrino mass states \(\nu_1, \nu_2, \nu_3\); in a frame component
  such as \(\mathcal{S}^{(i)}{}_{(i)}\) a direction \(x_i\) of ordinary
  space (the three components are equal); a repeated index is not summed
  unless a sum sign is written. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(r\): An extra-time direction of the fold: \(r \in \{5, 6, 7\}\)
  (\(x_5, x_6, x_7\)); not summed (the three components are equal).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(H\): The author's constant (an inverse length) that fixes the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\) of \(x_0\), the Lovelock couplings
  \(\alpha_{(p)} = w_{(p)}H^{2-2p}\) and the cosmological constant
  \(\Lambda = -7H^2\) (the author's original value \(-10H^2\) was
  corrected on 2026-10-25); in UM-3 a free parameter. Unit: GeV
  (1/length). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \({}'\): The derivative of a function of the time \(x_4\) alone with
  respect to \(x_4\) (\(a_4' = \mathrm{d}a_4/\mathrm{d}x_4\); two primes
  the second derivative); on a function of \(\sigma\) alone (a profile)
  the derivative with respect to \(\sigma\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\kappa_7\): The gravitational coupling of the fold: with the
  corrected Lovelock weights the thin source of the fold equals, to
  second order in the rates of expansion, the Einstein tensor of the
  seven-dimensional metric of the fold divided by
  \(\kappa_7 = 15\kappa H/16\), and exactly so in the Friedmann
  equations of a fold with frozen extra times; four-dimensional gravity
  on the fold has \(8\pi G_{\mathrm{N}} = \kappa_7/V_*\) (plus the
  induced term, if the fields of the fold induce one). Unit:
  GeV\(^{-5}\). Value: \(15\kappa H/16\). Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

The frozen extra times: the physical size \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4\) of
the extra times is constant while ordinary space expands. Then the fold
factor \(\mathcal{Z}\) is constant (IN-8) and every coupling of the
Standard Model is constant in time, as observation requires;
\(b_4 = c_b\,\mathrm{e}^{a_4}\) with a constant \(c_b\), so \(b_4\)
grows with the scale factor of ordinary space and is a non-trivial
function of \(x_4\), never identically 1, as the author requires. With
the corrected weights the bulk is then an exact vacuum for every
expansion history \(a_4(x_4)\), without any flux along \(\sigma\)
(DERIVED, \texttt{um3\_geometry.py}, G4; \texttt{um3\_fold.py}, J7;
\texttt{um3\_stabiliser.py}, ST9), and the fold carries the tension of
FE-32 plus matter with the energy density \(\rho_{\mathcal{S}}\) and the
pressure \(p_{\mathcal{S}}\) of the third line
(\(\rho_{\mathcal{S}} = -\mathcal{S}^{(4)}{}_{(4)} - 32H/(5\kappa)\) and
\(p_{\mathcal{S}} = \mathcal{S}^{(i)}{}_{(i)} + 32H/(5\kappa)\) in the
eight-dimensional normalisation): exactly the two Friedmann equations of
four-dimensional general relativity,
\(3a_4'^2 = \kappa_7\rho_{\mathcal{S}}\) and
\(-(2a_4'' + 3a_4'^2) = \kappa_7p_{\mathcal{S}}\), with the coupling
\(\kappa_7 = 15\kappa H/16\) and no correction of any order in
\(\mathsf{A}\). The matter of the fold is conserved by itself (the two
equations imply
\(\rho_{\mathcal{S}}' + 3a_4'(\rho_{\mathcal{S}} + p_{\mathcal{S}}) = 0\),
and there is no flux from the bulk). The component along the extra times
is a third condition: the matter above the tension must have the
pressure
\(-\frac45(8\mathsf{A}^2 + \mathsf{A}^4 + 4\mathsf{A}_1 + \mathsf{A}^2\mathsf{A}_1)H/\kappa\)
along the extra times, which radiation has (up to the term of order
\(\mathsf{A}^4\)) and dust and vacuum energy do not; the fold fluid
supplies the difference (FE-65), or else the extra times cannot stay
frozen (FE-72). Status: DERIVED.

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\kappa\,\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{bulk}}{}^{(4)}{}_{(4)} = \mathsf{A}^3(\mathsf{A} - \mathsf{U})^3H^2,\qquad
 \kappa\,\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{bulk}}{}^{(0)}{}_{(4)} = 3\mathsf{A}^2\mathsf{U}(\mathsf{A} - \mathsf{U})^2H^2\,\mathrm{sgn}(\sigma),\\
&\text{no bulk source:}\quad \mathsf{A} = 0\ \ \text{or}\ \ \mathsf{U} = \mathsf{A}\quad\text{at every instant}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-66}
\] \textbf{Symbols in (FE-66).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\kappa\): The eight-dimensional gravitational coupling,
  \(\kappa = 8\pi G_8\). Unit: GeV\(^{-6}\). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{bulk}}\): The energy-momentum tensor of all
  sources of the bulk (with the corrected weights dirac16complex alone,
  \(\mathcal{T}_\Psi\); with the author's original weights also the
  stabiliser); \(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{bulk}}{}^{(0)}{}_b\) is its flux
  along \(\sigma\) (frame components). Unit: GeV\(^8\). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathsf{A}\): The expansion rate of ordinary space in units of
  \(H\), \(\mathsf{A} = a_4'/H\). Unit: dimensionless. Value: 0 at the
  static block; \(H_4^{(0)}/H\) today (\(H_4^{(0)}\) the Hubble
  constant); 1 at the author's vacuum, which is not a solution with the
  corrected weights.
\item
  \(\mathsf{U}\): The rate of change of \(b_4\) in units of \(H\),
  \(\mathsf{U} = b_4'/(Hb_4)\). Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(\mathsf{U} = \mathsf{A}\) with frozen extra times.
\item
  \(H\): The author's constant (an inverse length) that fixes the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\) of \(x_0\), the Lovelock couplings
  \(\alpha_{(p)} = w_{(p)}H^{2-2p}\) and the cosmological constant
  \(\Lambda = -7H^2\) (the author's original value \(-10H^2\) was
  corrected on 2026-10-25); in UM-3 a free parameter. Unit: GeV
  (1/length). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathrm{sgn}\): The sign function: \(\mathrm{sgn}(y) = +1\) for
  \(y > 0\), \(-1\) for \(y < 0\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\sigma\): The proper coordinate of the hidden direction:
  \(\mathrm{d}\sigma = \lvert\cot z\rvert\,\mathrm{d}x_0\),
  \(\sigma = \mathrm{sgn}(\pi/2 - z)\ln(\sin z)/(6H)\) on one period
  \(0 < z < \pi\); \(\sigma = 0\) is the fold \(z = \pi/2\),
  \(\sigma \to \mp\infty\) the tips \(z \to 0, \pi\); one period of
  \(x_0\) is the whole line \(-\infty < \sigma < \infty\). Unit: length.
  Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

The two constraints of the bulk equations of the author's metric, for
any \(a_4(x_4)\) and \(b_4(x_4)\): the \((x_4, x_4)\) component (the
energy constraint) and the \((\sigma, x_4)\) component (the flow of
energy along the hidden direction) contain no second time derivative
(\(\mathsf{A}_1\) and \(\mathsf{U}_1\) drop out), so they hold at every
instant for the rates of that instant. Without a bulk source the energy
constraint requires \(\mathsf{A} = 0\) or \(\mathsf{U} = \mathsf{A}\) at
every instant, and on both branches every other component vanishes as
well (FE-29): a bulk without any source has, at every instant, either
static ordinary space or frozen extra times. The expanding fold of our
universe is on the second branch. With the author's original weights the
energy constraint was
\(\kappa\mathcal{T}^{(4)}{}_{(4)} = (H^2/16)(5\mathsf{A}^6 - 15\mathsf{A}^5\mathsf{U} + 15\mathsf{A}^4\mathsf{U}^2 - 9\mathsf{A}^4 - 5\mathsf{A}^3\mathsf{U}^3 + 18\mathsf{A}^3\mathsf{U} + 3\mathsf{A}^2 - 9\mathsf{A}\mathsf{U}^3 - 3\mathsf{A}\mathsf{U} - 3\mathsf{U}^2 + 1)\);
together with the flux it allowed a sourceless bulk only at the author's
vacuum \((\mathsf{A}, \mathsf{U}) = (\pm1, 0)\), and a slowly expanding
bulk needed a source of the order \(H^2/\kappa\). Status: DERIVED
(\texttt{um3\_background.py}, BG1 and BG2; \texttt{um3\_stabiliser.py},
ST3, ST8 and ST9; \texttt{um3\_geometry.py}, G4).

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\kappa\,\mathcal{S}^a{}_b + \frac{32}{5}H\,\delta^a{}_b = \frac{\kappa}{\kappa_7}\,\mathcal{G}_{\mathrm{F}}{}^a{}_b
 + (\text{terms of fourth order in } \mathsf{A}, \mathsf{V}),\qquad \mathsf{V} = \mathsf{U} - \mathsf{A},\\
&\frac{\mathcal{G}_{\mathrm{F}}{}^{(4)}{}_{(4)}}{H^2} = -3\big(\mathsf{A}^2 + 3\mathsf{A}\mathsf{V} + \mathsf{V}^2\big),\qquad
 \frac{\mathcal{G}_{\mathrm{F}}{}^{(i)}{}_{(i)}}{H^2} = -\big(3\mathsf{A}^2 + 6\mathsf{A}\mathsf{V} + 6\mathsf{V}^2 + 2\mathsf{A}_1 + 3\mathsf{V}_1\big),\\
&\frac{\mathcal{G}_{\mathrm{F}}{}^{(r)}{}_{(r)}}{H^2} = -\big(6\mathsf{A}^2 + 6\mathsf{A}\mathsf{V} + 3\mathsf{V}^2 + 3\mathsf{A}_1 + 2\mathsf{V}_1\big)
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-71}
\] \textbf{Symbols in (FE-71).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\kappa\): The eight-dimensional gravitational coupling,
  \(\kappa = 8\pi G_8\). Unit: GeV\(^{-6}\). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathcal{S}^a{}_b\): The thin source of the fold: the
  energy-momentum tensor concentrated on the fold, integrated across it,
  \(\kappa\mathcal{S}^a{}_b = \int_{0^-}^{0^+}\mathrm{d}\sigma\sum_p\alpha_{(p)}\mathcal{E}_{(p)}{}^a{}_b\)
  (FD-11, the junction conditions); \(\mathcal{S}^4{}_4\) is minus its
  energy density, \(\mathcal{S}^1{}_1\) and \(\mathcal{S}^5{}_5\) its
  pressures along ordinary space and along the extra times (frame
  components along the fold, eight-dimensional normalisation: an energy
  per unit volume of the six other directions of the fold). Unit:
  GeV\(^7\). Value:
  \(\kappa\mathcal{S}^a{}_b = -\frac{32}{5}H\delta^a{}_b\) at the static
  block. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(a\): A frame (orthonormal) index running over \(0, 1, \dots, 7\)
  (frame direction \((0)\) along \(\sigma\), \((1), (2), (3)\) along
  ordinary space, \((4)\) along the time, \((5), (6), (7)\) along the
  extra times); raised and lowered with the frame metric
  \(\eta_{ab} = \mathrm{diag}(1, 1, 1, 1, -1, -1, -1, -1)\); on the fold
  (\(a, b \in \{1, \dots, 7\}\)) the frame of the fold; a repeated index
  is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(b\): A frame index (as \(a\)). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(H\): The author's constant (an inverse length) that fixes the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\) of \(x_0\), the Lovelock couplings
  \(\alpha_{(p)} = w_{(p)}H^{2-2p}\) and the cosmological constant
  \(\Lambda = -7H^2\) (the author's original value \(-10H^2\) was
  corrected on 2026-10-25); in UM-3 a free parameter. Unit: GeV
  (1/length). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\delta\): The Kronecker delta: with two indices \(\delta^M_N = 1\)
  if \(M = N\) and 0 otherwise (likewise \(\delta_{X\mathrm{L}}\) is 1
  if the label \(X\) of a half of dirac16complex is L and 0 otherwise);
  with upper and lower index strings the generalised Kronecker delta
  \(\delta^{M_1\cdots M_n}_{N_1\cdots N_n}\), the determinant of the
  \(n \times n\) matrix of the Kronecker deltas \(\delta^{M_a}_{N_b}\)
  (\(+1\) or \(-1\) if the upper indices are an even or odd permutation
  of the lower ones, 0 otherwise). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\kappa_7\): The gravitational coupling of the fold: with the
  corrected Lovelock weights the thin source of the fold equals, to
  second order in the rates of expansion, the Einstein tensor of the
  seven-dimensional metric of the fold divided by
  \(\kappa_7 = 15\kappa H/16\), and exactly so in the Friedmann
  equations of a fold with frozen extra times; four-dimensional gravity
  on the fold has \(8\pi G_{\mathrm{N}} = \kappa_7/V_*\) (plus the
  induced term, if the fields of the fold induce one). Unit:
  GeV\(^{-5}\). Value: \(15\kappa H/16\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathcal{G}_{\mathrm{F}}{}^a{}_b\): The Einstein tensor of the
  seven-dimensional metric of the fold (FD-2), frame components along
  the fold. Unit: 1/length\(^2\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathsf{A}\): The expansion rate of ordinary space in units of
  \(H\), \(\mathsf{A} = a_4'/H\). Unit: dimensionless. Value: 0 at the
  static block; \(H_4^{(0)}/H\) today (\(H_4^{(0)}\) the Hubble
  constant); 1 at the author's vacuum, which is not a solution with the
  corrected weights.
\item
  \(\mathsf{V}\): The rate of change of the scale factor
  \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4\) of the extra times on the fold in units of
  \(H\), \(\mathsf{V} = \mathsf{U} - \mathsf{A}\); zero with frozen
  extra times. Unit: dimensionless.
\item
  \(\mathsf{U}\): The rate of change of \(b_4\) in units of \(H\),
  \(\mathsf{U} = b_4'/(Hb_4)\). Unit: dimensionless. Value:
  \(\mathsf{U} = \mathsf{A}\) with frozen extra times.
\item
  \(i\): A generation index (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)), which also labels the
  neutrino mass states \(\nu_1, \nu_2, \nu_3\); in a frame component
  such as \(\mathcal{S}^{(i)}{}_{(i)}\) a direction \(x_i\) of ordinary
  space (the three components are equal); a repeated index is not summed
  unless a sum sign is written. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathsf{A}_1\): The time derivative of \(\mathsf{A}\) in units of
  \(H\), \(\mathsf{A}_1 = \mathsf{A}'/H = a_4''/H^2\). Unit:
  dimensionless.
\item
  \(\mathsf{V}_1\): The time derivative of \(\mathsf{V}\) in units of
  \(H\), \(\mathsf{V}_1 = \mathsf{V}'/H\). Unit: dimensionless.
\item
  \(r\): An extra-time direction of the fold: \(r \in \{5, 6, 7\}\)
  (\(x_5, x_6, x_7\)); not summed (the three components are equal).
  Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

The fold as a seven-dimensional Einstein brane: for any \(a_4(x_4)\) and
\(b_4(x_4)\) the source of the fold (FE-30) minus its static tension
equals, to second order in the rates (\(\mathsf{A}\) and the rate
\(\mathsf{V}\) of the scale factor \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4\) of the extra
times of first order, \(\mathsf{A}_1\) and
\(\mathsf{V}_1 = \mathsf{V}'/H\) of second order), \(1/\kappa_7\) times
the Einstein tensor \(\mathcal{G}_{\mathrm{F}}\) of the
seven-dimensional metric of the fold (FD-2), whose frame components are
the second and third lines. The fold therefore obeys Einstein's
equations in seven dimensions with the coupling
\(\kappa_7 = 15\kappa H/16\), the Einstein-Hilbert term that the
Gauss-Bonnet and cubic terms of the bulk give the kink of the metric
(the induced gravity of brane worlds with Lovelock terms; S. C. Davis,
Phys. Rev.~D 67 (2003) 024030; E. Gravanis and S. Willison, Phys. Lett.
B 562 (2003) 118). The remainders start at the fourth order (along the
time
\(-\frac{12}{5}\mathsf{A}(\mathsf{A} - \mathsf{U})(\mathsf{A}^2 - \mathsf{A}\mathsf{U} - \mathsf{U}^2)H\));
for frozen extra times (\(\mathsf{V} = 0\)) the components along
ordinary space and the time hold exactly (FE-33), the component along
the extra times keeps
\(-\frac45\mathsf{A}^2(\mathsf{A}^2 + \mathsf{A}_1)H\). Integrated over
the extra times, four-dimensional gravity on the fold has
\(8\pi G_{\mathrm{N}} = \kappa_7/V_*\) (with the induced term IN-9,
\(1/(8\pi G_{\mathrm{N}}) = V_*/\kappa_7 + 1/\kappa_{\mathrm{ind}}\)).
Status: DERIVED (\texttt{um3\_series.py}, SR4).

\[
H^2\big(\mathsf{V}_1 + 3\mathsf{V}(\mathsf{A} + \mathsf{V})\big) = \frac{8\pi G_{\mathrm{N}}}{5}\big(\rho - 3\mathcal{P} + 2q_{\mathrm{tot}}\big) \tag{FE-72}
\] \textbf{Symbols in (FE-72).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(H\): The author's constant (an inverse length) that fixes the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\) of \(x_0\), the Lovelock couplings
  \(\alpha_{(p)} = w_{(p)}H^{2-2p}\) and the cosmological constant
  \(\Lambda = -7H^2\) (the author's original value \(-10H^2\) was
  corrected on 2026-10-25); in UM-3 a free parameter. Unit: GeV
  (1/length). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathsf{V}_1\): The time derivative of \(\mathsf{V}\) in units of
  \(H\), \(\mathsf{V}_1 = \mathsf{V}'/H\). Unit: dimensionless.
\item
  \(\mathsf{V}\): The rate of change of the scale factor
  \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4\) of the extra times on the fold in units of
  \(H\), \(\mathsf{V} = \mathsf{U} - \mathsf{A}\); zero with frozen
  extra times. Unit: dimensionless.
\item
  \(\mathsf{A}\): The expansion rate of ordinary space in units of
  \(H\), \(\mathsf{A} = a_4'/H\). Unit: dimensionless. Value: 0 at the
  static block; \(H_4^{(0)}/H\) today (\(H_4^{(0)}\) the Hubble
  constant); 1 at the author's vacuum, which is not a solution with the
  corrected weights.
\item
  \(\pi\): The number pi, the ratio of the circumference of a circle to
  its diameter. Unit: dimensionless. Value: 3.141592654. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(G_{\mathrm{N}}\): Newton's constant of four-dimensional gravity (the
  gravity of our world, on the fold),
  \(G_{\mathrm{N}} = 1/M_{\mathrm{Pl}}^2\) with the Planck mass
  \(M_{\mathrm{Pl}} = 1.221\times10^{19}\) GeV. Unit: GeV\(^{-2}\).
  Value: \(6.708\times10^{-39}\) GeV\(^{-2}\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\rho\): The total energy density of the matter of the fold (all
  species); \(\Delta\rho\) its perturbation. Unit: GeV\(^4\). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{P}\): The total pressure of the matter of the fold;
  \(\Delta\mathcal{P}\) its perturbation. Unit: GeV\(^4\). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(q_{\mathrm{tot}}\): The total pressure along the extra times of the
  matter of the fold (the Standard Model, the bound states of
  dirac16complex, the portals and the fold fluid without its tension).
  Unit: GeV\(^4\). Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

The equation of the volume of the extra times (the radion): the
combination of the three components of FE-71 that eliminates
\(\mathsf{A}_1\) and the energy density, with \(\rho\), \(\mathcal{P}\)
and \(q_{\mathrm{tot}}\) the energy density, the pressure along ordinary
space and the pressure along the extra times of all matter of the fold
above its static tension (four-dimensional normalisation). Radiation
does not drive the volume (\(\rho = 3\mathcal{P}\), \(q = 0\)); dust and
vacuum energy do. The extra times stay frozen (\(\mathsf{V} = 0\)) only
if \(\rho - 3\mathcal{P} + 2q_{\mathrm{tot}} = 0\), which is the
condition that the fold fluid fulfils with \(q_{\mathrm{hold}}\)
(FE-65). Without it, from the radiation era to today with the observed
content (dark matter \(\chi\) with \(q = 0\), baryons with
\(q = -\rho\), vacuum energy with \(q = -\rho\)), the scale factor of
the extra times grows by \(\mathrm{e}^{0.92}\), and since
\(8\pi G_{\mathrm{N}} = \kappa_7/(V_{\mathrm{t}}(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4)^3)\)
and every coupling of the fold is divided by \(\mathcal{Z}\) (FE-44),
Newton's constant at nucleosynthesis would have been 15.8 times its
present value (observed: \(1.01^{+0.20}_{-0.16}\); C. J. Copi, A. N.
Davis and L. M. Krauss, Phys. Rev.~Lett. 92 (2004) 171301), and today
\(G_{\mathrm{N}}\) and the fine-structure constant would change by
\(-6.5\times10^{-11}\) per year, 6800 times the bound of lunar laser
ranging (\((-5.0 \pm 9.6)\times10^{-15}\) per year; L. Biskupek, J.
Müller and J.-M. Torre, Universe 7 (2021) 34) and \(2.6\times10^8\)
times that of optical clocks (\((1.8 \pm 2.5)\times10^{-19}\) per year;
M. Filzinger et al., Phys. Rev.~Lett. 130 (2023) 253001). The volume of
the extra times must be held fixed. Status: DERIVED
(\texttt{um3\_series.py}, SR4 and SR5).

\hypertarget{the-general-warped-metric-and-the-power-series-of-the-metric}{%
\subsection{The general warped metric and the power series of the
metric}\label{the-general-warped-metric-and-the-power-series-of-the-metric}}

\[
\begin{aligned}
&g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N = \mathrm{d}\sigma^2 + \mathrm{e}^{2\mathfrak{a}}\big(\mathrm{d}x_1^2 + \mathrm{d}x_2^2 + \mathrm{d}x_3^2\big)
 - \mathrm{e}^{2\mathfrak{n}}\,\mathrm{d}x_4^2 - \mathrm{e}^{2\mathfrak{b}}\big(\mathrm{d}x_5^2 + \mathrm{d}x_6^2 + \mathrm{d}x_7^2\big),\\
&R^{(0)(i)}{}_{(0)(i)} = -\partial_\sigma^2\mathfrak{a} - (\partial_\sigma\mathfrak{a})^2,\qquad
 R^{(0)(4)}{}_{(0)(4)} = -\partial_\sigma^2\mathfrak{n} - (\partial_\sigma\mathfrak{n})^2,\qquad
 R^{(i)(j)}{}_{(i)(j)} = -(\partial_\sigma\mathfrak{a})^2 + \mathrm{e}^{-2\mathfrak{n}}(\partial_{x_4}\mathfrak{a})^2,\\
&R^{(i)(r)}{}_{(i)(r)} = -\partial_\sigma\mathfrak{a}\,\partial_\sigma\mathfrak{b} + \mathrm{e}^{-2\mathfrak{n}}\,\partial_{x_4}\mathfrak{a}\,\partial_{x_4}\mathfrak{b},\qquad
 R^{(i)(4)}{}_{(i)(4)} = -\partial_\sigma\mathfrak{a}\,\partial_\sigma\mathfrak{n} + \mathrm{e}^{-2\mathfrak{n}}\big(\partial_{x_4}^2\mathfrak{a}
 + (\partial_{x_4}\mathfrak{a})^2 - \partial_{x_4}\mathfrak{a}\,\partial_{x_4}\mathfrak{n}\big),\\
&R^{(0)(i)}{}_{(4)(i)} = -\mathrm{e}^{-\mathfrak{n}}\big(\partial_\sigma\partial_{x_4}\mathfrak{a} + \partial_\sigma\mathfrak{a}\,\partial_{x_4}\mathfrak{a}
 - \partial_{x_4}\mathfrak{a}\,\partial_\sigma\mathfrak{n}\big)
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-34}
\] \textbf{Symbols in (FE-34).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval,
  \(g^{MN}\) its inverse, \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its
  determinant (\(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper
  volume element). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(X = \{x_0, \dots, x_7\}\): The author's coordinates of the
  eight-dimensional spacetime, \(X = \{x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\}\) with the
  hidden direction first (Dirac\_Einstein-Lovelock\_4+4\_Nash.nb,
  section DEFCHART); \(X^M\) is the coordinate with the index \(M\) and
  \(\mathrm{d}^8X = \mathrm{d}x_0\,\mathrm{d}x_1\cdots\mathrm{d}x_7\)
  the coordinate volume element. Unit: length (each coordinate). Status:
  AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\sigma\): The proper coordinate of the hidden direction:
  \(\mathrm{d}\sigma = \lvert\cot z\rvert\,\mathrm{d}x_0\),
  \(\sigma = \mathrm{sgn}(\pi/2 - z)\ln(\sin z)/(6H)\) on one period
  \(0 < z < \pi\); \(\sigma = 0\) is the fold \(z = \pi/2\),
  \(\sigma \to \mp\infty\) the tips \(z \to 0, \pi\); one period of
  \(x_0\) is the whole line \(-\infty < \sigma < \infty\). Unit: length.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathfrak{a},\ \mathfrak{a}_1\): The logarithm of the scale factor
  of ordinary space in the general warped metric FE-34, a function of
  \(\sigma\) and \(x_4\); for the author's metric
  \(\mathfrak{a} = a_4 - H\lvert\sigma\rvert\); \(\mathfrak{a}_n\) its
  term of order \(n\) in the power series FE-35 (\(\mathfrak{a}_1\) the
  first-order term). Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(x_1\): The first coordinate of ordinary 3-space. Unit: length.
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_2\): The second coordinate of ordinary 3-space. Unit: length.
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_3\): The third coordinate of ordinary 3-space. Unit: length.
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathfrak{n},\ \mathfrak{n}_1\): The logarithm of the lapse in the
  general warped metric FE-34
  (\(g_{x_4x_4} = -\mathrm{e}^{2\mathfrak{n}}\)); for the author's
  metric \(\mathfrak{n} = 0\); \(\mathfrak{n}_n\) its term of order
  \(n\) (\(\mathfrak{n}_1\) the first-order term). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; a prime on a function of \(x_4\) alone is
  \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit: length (= time, \(c = 1\)).
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathfrak{b},\ \mathfrak{b}_1\): The logarithm of the scale factor
  of the extra times in the general warped metric FE-34; for the
  author's metric
  \(\mathfrak{b} = \ln b_4 - a_4 - H\lvert\sigma\rvert\);
  \(\mathfrak{b}_n\) its term of order \(n\) (\(\mathfrak{b}_1\) the
  first-order term). Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(x_5\): The first of the three extra time-like coordinates. Unit:
  length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_6\): The second of the three extra time-like coordinates. Unit:
  length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(x_7\): The third of the three extra time-like coordinates. Unit:
  length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(R^{MN}{}_{PQ},\ R_{MN},\ R\): The curvature of the metric
  \(g_{MN}\): with four indices the Riemann tensor (convention
  \([\nabla_P, \nabla_Q]V^M = R^M{}_{NPQ}V^N\)), with two indices the
  Ricci tensor \(R_{MN} = R^P{}_{MPN}\), without indices the scalar
  curvature \(R = g^{MN}R_{MN}\); with frame indices
  \(R^{(a)(b)}{}_{(c)(d)}\) its frame components,
  \(R^{(a)(b)}{}_{(a)(b)}\) (no sum) the sectional curvature of the
  frame plane \((a, b)\). Unit: 1/length\(^2\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(i\): A generation index (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)), which also labels the
  neutrino mass states \(\nu_1, \nu_2, \nu_3\); in a frame component
  such as \(\mathcal{S}^{(i)}{}_{(i)}\) a direction \(x_i\) of ordinary
  space (the three components are equal); a repeated index is not summed
  unless a sum sign is written. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(j\): A second generation or mass-state index (as \(i\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(r\): An extra-time direction of the fold: \(r \in \{5, 6, 7\}\)
  (\(x_5, x_6, x_7\)); not summed (the three components are equal).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\partial\): A partial derivative: \(\partial_M\) with respect to the
  coordinate \(X^M\), \(\partial_\mu\) with respect to \(x^\mu\),
  \(\partial_\sigma\) with respect to \(\sigma\),
  \(\partial X/\partial Y\) the derivative of \(X\) with respect to
  \(Y\) at fixed other variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\sigma\): The label \(\sigma\) in \(\partial_\sigma\): the
  derivative with respect to the proper hidden coordinate \(\sigma\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

The general metric with the symmetries of the author's metric
(homogeneous and isotropic in ordinary space and in the extra times,
independent of the extra times) but with three arbitrary functions
\(\mathfrak{a}\), \(\mathfrak{b}\), \(\mathfrak{n}\) of \(\sigma\) and
\(x_4\), and its frame curvature: the components with the extra times
follow from those with ordinary space by replacing \(\mathfrak{a}\) by
\(\mathfrak{b}\) and \((i)\) by \((r)\) (\(R^{(0)(r)}{}_{(0)(r)}\),
\(R^{(r)(s)}{}_{(r)(s)}\), \(R^{(r)(4)}{}_{(r)(4)}\),
\(R^{(0)(r)}{}_{(4)(r)}\)); all others vanish (DERIVED,
\texttt{um3\_fold.py}, J2). Its Lovelock tensor is FE-2 with these
components; it has only diagonal components and the \((\sigma, x_4)\)
pair. The author's metric is
\(\mathfrak{a} = a_4 - H\lvert\sigma\rvert\),
\(\mathfrak{b} = \ln b_4 - a_4 - H\lvert\sigma\rvert\),
\(\mathfrak{n} = 0\), and the general equations reduce to FE-29 on both
sides of the fold (DERIVED, J2). Status: DERIVED.

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\mathfrak{a} = a_4 - H\lvert\sigma\rvert + \sum_{n\ge1}\epsilon_{\mathrm{b}}^{\,n}\,\mathfrak{a}_n(\sigma, x_4),\qquad
 \mathfrak{b} = \ln b_4 - a_4 - H\lvert\sigma\rvert + \sum_{n\ge1}\epsilon_{\mathrm{b}}^{\,n}\,\mathfrak{b}_n(\sigma, x_4),\\
&\mathfrak{n} = \sum_{n\ge1}\epsilon_{\mathrm{b}}^{\,n}\,\mathfrak{n}_n(\sigma, x_4),\qquad
 \hat h_{\mu\nu} \to \hat h_{\mu\nu} + \epsilon_{\mathrm{c}}\,\hat h^{(1)}_{\mu\nu} + \dots
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-35}
\] \textbf{Symbols in (FE-35).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\mathfrak{a},\ \mathfrak{a}_1\): The logarithm of the scale factor
  of ordinary space in the general warped metric FE-34, a function of
  \(\sigma\) and \(x_4\); for the author's metric
  \(\mathfrak{a} = a_4 - H\lvert\sigma\rvert\); \(\mathfrak{a}_n\) its
  term of order \(n\) in the power series FE-35 (\(\mathfrak{a}_1\) the
  first-order term). Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(a_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) that scales
  ordinary 3-space: on the fold \(\mathrm{e}^{a_4}\) is the scale factor
  of cosmology and \(a_4'\) the Hubble rate. Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(H\): The author's constant (an inverse length) that fixes the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\) of \(x_0\), the Lovelock couplings
  \(\alpha_{(p)} = w_{(p)}H^{2-2p}\) and the cosmological constant
  \(\Lambda = -7H^2\) (the author's original value \(-10H^2\) was
  corrected on 2026-10-25); in UM-3 a free parameter. Unit: GeV
  (1/length). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\sigma\): The proper coordinate of the hidden direction:
  \(\mathrm{d}\sigma = \lvert\cot z\rvert\,\mathrm{d}x_0\),
  \(\sigma = \mathrm{sgn}(\pi/2 - z)\ln(\sin z)/(6H)\) on one period
  \(0 < z < \pi\); \(\sigma = 0\) is the fold \(z = \pi/2\),
  \(\sigma \to \mp\infty\) the tips \(z \to 0, \pi\); one period of
  \(x_0\) is the whole line \(-\infty < \sigma < \infty\). Unit: length.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(n\): The order \(n = 1, 2, \dots\) of a term of the power series of
  the metric (also its exponent of \(\epsilon_{\mathrm{r}}\));
  \(\mathfrak{a}_n\), \(\mathfrak{b}_n\), \(\mathfrak{n}_n\) are the
  terms of order \(n\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\epsilon_{\mathrm{b}}\): The bookkeeping parameter of the
  deformations of the metric along \(\sigma\) that sources depending on
  \(\sigma\) require (their amplitude relative to the order-0 metric of
  the author). Unit: dimensionless.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; a prime on a function of \(x_4\) alone is
  \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit: length (= time, \(c = 1\)).
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathfrak{b},\ \mathfrak{b}_1\): The logarithm of the scale factor
  of the extra times in the general warped metric FE-34; for the
  author's metric
  \(\mathfrak{b} = \ln b_4 - a_4 - H\lvert\sigma\rvert\);
  \(\mathfrak{b}_n\) its term of order \(n\) (\(\mathfrak{b}_1\) the
  first-order term). Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(b_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) in the
  extra-time components, \(b_4 > 0\), never identically 1 (the author's
  order of 2026-10-24); the scale factor (the factor of the lengths) of
  the extra times is \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4\) on the fold and
  \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4\,\mathrm{e}^{-H\lvert\sigma\rvert}\) at the
  distance \(\sigma\) from it (the square root of the warp factor times
  \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathfrak{n},\ \mathfrak{n}_1\): The logarithm of the lapse in the
  general warped metric FE-34
  (\(g_{x_4x_4} = -\mathrm{e}^{2\mathfrak{n}}\)); for the author's
  metric \(\mathfrak{n} = 0\); \(\mathfrak{n}_n\) its term of order
  \(n\) (\(\mathfrak{n}_1\) the first-order term). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\hat h_{\mu\nu}\): The four-dimensional metric of the fold,
  \(\hat h_{\mu\nu} = \mathrm{diag}(\mathrm{e}^{2a_4}, \mathrm{e}^{2a_4}, \mathrm{e}^{2a_4}, -1)\)
  in the coordinates \(x_1, \dots, x_4\) (the induced metric at
  \(\sigma = 0\) without the extra times); \(\hat h^{\mu\nu}\) its
  inverse, \(\hat h\) its determinant,
  \(\sqrt{-\hat h} = \mathrm{e}^{3a_4}\); \(\hat h^{(1)}_{\mu\nu}\) its
  first-order cosmological perturbation. Unit: dimensionless. Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mu\): A four-dimensional coordinate index running over the
  observable coordinates \(x_1, x_2, x_3, x_4\) of the fold (signature
  \(+,+,+,-\)); a repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\nu\): A four-dimensional coordinate index (as \(\mu\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\epsilon_{\mathrm{c}}\): The bookkeeping parameter of the
  cosmological perturbations of the metric of the fold (the size of the
  CMB anisotropies). Unit: dimensionless. Value: \(\sim 10^{-5}\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\end{itemize}

The metric as a power series of terms of decreasing magnitude (the
author's request): the order-0 term is the author's metric; the terms of
order \(n\) in \(\epsilon_{\mathrm{b}}\) are the deformations of the
metric along \(\sigma\) that sources depending on \(\sigma\) (the matter
of the fold away from the frozen branch, dirac16complex) require;
\(\epsilon_{\mathrm{c}} \sim 10^{-5}\) (the size of the CMB
anisotropies) counts the cosmological perturbations of the metric of the
fold. With the corrected weights the static block is a degenerate vacuum
(FE-70), and the scheme is not a regular power series: the linearised
static bulk equations vanish identically on both sides of the fold, so
the first-order terms obey no equation at first order; at second order
the \(\sigma\) component requires
\(\mathfrak{a}_1'^2 + 3\mathfrak{a}_1'\mathfrak{b}_1' + \mathfrak{b}_1'^2 = 0\)
(the prime here the derivative with respect to \(\sigma\)), and the
components along ordinary space and the extra times then force every
non-trivial static deformation to grow like
\(\mathrm{e}^{3H\lvert\sigma\rvert}\) away from the fold, so there is no
bounded static correction; and the source of the fold does not respond
at first order to jumps of the first \(\sigma\)-derivatives. The
perturbations of the bulk are strongly coupled: their amplitude is fixed
only at second order (DERIVED, \texttt{um3\_series.py}, SR1 to SR3), and
how the bulk responds to a source that depends on \(\sigma\) is OPEN.
The cosmology of the fold does not need this response: with frozen extra
times the order-0 metric is an exact solution of the bulk equations for
any \(a_4(x_4)\), and the fold obeys the Friedmann equations exactly
(FE-33). Status: DERIVED (the scheme and the degeneracy), OPEN (the
response of the strongly coupled bulk).

\hypertarget{dirac16complex-modes-and-kohn-sham-equations}{%
\section{dirac16complex: modes and Kohn-Sham
equations}\label{dirac16complex-modes-and-kohn-sham-equations}}

dirac16complex lives in the bulk; its four-dimensional particles are its
modes along the hidden direction. This section writes the equation of
those modes on the static block (the late universe; an expanding fold
replaces \(a_4\), \(b_4\) by their values at the time), the conditions
at the fold, the states bound to the fold, and the Kohn-Sham equations
that make the description self-consistent with the self-interaction,
with gravity and with the mean fields of the Standard Model.

\hypertarget{the-equation-of-the-hidden-direction}{%
\subsection{The equation of the hidden
direction}\label{the-equation-of-the-hidden-direction}}

\[
\Psi = \mathrm{e}^{3H\lvert\sigma\rvert}\,\chi(\sigma)\,\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}(\vec k\cdot\vec x - \omega x_4)},\qquad
\gamma^{(0)}\chi' - \mathrm{i}\omega\,\gamma^{(4)}\chi + \mathrm{i}\,\mathrm{e}^{H\lvert\sigma\rvert}\,\vec k\cdot\vec\gamma\,\chi
 - m_\Psi\chi = 0\qquad(\sigma \ne 0) \tag{FE-36}
\] \textbf{Symbols in (FE-36).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\Psi,\ \bar\Psi\): dirac16complex: the author's 16-component complex
  spinor field of eight dimensions;
  \(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger\sigma_{16}\) is its Dirac adjoint; in a term
  of the fold, \(\Psi\) is its value at \(\sigma = 0\). Unit:
  GeV\(^{7/2}\). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(H\): The author's constant (an inverse length) that fixes the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\) of \(x_0\), the Lovelock couplings
  \(\alpha_{(p)} = w_{(p)}H^{2-2p}\) and the cosmological constant
  \(\Lambda = -7H^2\) (the author's original value \(-10H^2\) was
  corrected on 2026-10-25); in UM-3 a free parameter. Unit: GeV
  (1/length). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\sigma\): The proper coordinate of the hidden direction:
  \(\mathrm{d}\sigma = \lvert\cot z\rvert\,\mathrm{d}x_0\),
  \(\sigma = \mathrm{sgn}(\pi/2 - z)\ln(\sin z)/(6H)\) on one period
  \(0 < z < \pi\); \(\sigma = 0\) is the fold \(z = \pi/2\),
  \(\sigma \to \mp\infty\) the tips \(z \to 0, \pi\); one period of
  \(x_0\) is the whole line \(-\infty < \sigma < \infty\). Unit: length.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\chi(\sigma)\): The profile of a mode of dirac16complex along the
  hidden direction,
  \(\Psi = \mathrm{e}^{3H\lvert\sigma\rvert}\chi(\sigma)\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}(\vec k\cdot\vec x - \omega x_4)}\)
  (a 16-component spinor function of \(\sigma\)); as a four-dimensional
  field, \(\chi\) denotes the particle of a bound state. Unit:
  GeV\(^{2}\) (with the \(V_*\) normalisation). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\vec k\): The (comoving) momentum along ordinary space, \(\vec k^2\)
  its square. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\vec x\): The position in ordinary space,
  \(\vec x = (x_1, x_2, x_3)\). Unit: length. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\omega\): A frequency: the energy of a mode
  \(\propto \mathrm{e}^{-\mathrm{i}\omega x_4}\) (\(\omega_o\) of the
  orbital \(o\)). Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(x_4\): The time; a prime on a function of \(x_4\) alone is
  \(\mathrm{d}/\mathrm{d}x_4\). Unit: length (= time, \(c = 1\)).
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\gamma^M,\ \gamma^{(a)}\): The Dirac matrices of dirac16complex
  along the coordinates, \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^MT^a\),
  \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\); \(\gamma^{(a)} = T^a\) along the frame
  direction \((a)\) (\(\gamma^{(0)}\) along \(\sigma\), \(\gamma^{(4)}\)
  along the time). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \({}'\): The derivative of a function of the time \(x_4\) alone with
  respect to \(x_4\) (\(a_4' = \mathrm{d}a_4/\mathrm{d}x_4\); two primes
  the second derivative); on a function of \(\sigma\) alone (a profile)
  the derivative with respect to \(\sigma\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\vec\gamma\): The three frame Dirac matrices of dirac16complex along
  ordinary space,
  \(\vec\gamma = (\gamma^{(1)}, \gamma^{(2)}, \gamma^{(3)})\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(m_\Psi\): The mass parameter of dirac16complex in the bulk. Unit:
  GeV. Status: RECORD.
\end{itemize}

The free field equation FE-12 on the static block (\(a_4\) and \(b_4\)
constant, ordinary space normalised so that \(\mathrm{e}^{a_4} = 1\) at
the fold) for a mode of definite momentum \(\vec k\) along ordinary
space and frequency \(\omega\), independent of the extra times. The
factor \(\mathrm{e}^{3H\lvert\sigma\rvert}\) removes the spin connection
exactly
(\(\gamma^{(0)}\partial_\sigma\mathrm{e}^{3H\lvert\sigma\rvert} = 3H\,\mathrm{sgn}(\sigma)\gamma^{(0)}\mathrm{e}^{3H\lvert\sigma\rvert}\)
cancels the first term of FE-14), so that the profile \(\chi(\sigma)\)
obeys a Dirac equation of one variable whose only dependence on
\(\sigma\) is the factor \(\mathrm{e}^{H\lvert\sigma\rvert}\) (one over
the square root of the warp factor) of the momentum. The
four-dimensional norm density is
\(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\Psi^\dagger\Psi = \chi^\dagger\chi\) (times
\(b_4^3\)). Status: DERIVED.

\[
\chi' = \mathbb{K}\,\chi,\qquad \mathbb{K} = \gamma^{(0)}\big(m_\Psi + \mathrm{i}\omega\gamma^{(4)}\big),\qquad
\mathbb{K}^2 = \big(m_\Psi^2 - \omega^2\big)\mathbf{1},\qquad \chi \propto \mathrm{e}^{\pm q\sigma},\qquad q = \sqrt{m_\Psi^2 - \omega^2} \tag{FE-37}
\] \textbf{Symbols in (FE-37).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\chi(\sigma)\): The profile of a mode of dirac16complex along the
  hidden direction,
  \(\Psi = \mathrm{e}^{3H\lvert\sigma\rvert}\chi(\sigma)\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}(\vec k\cdot\vec x - \omega x_4)}\)
  (a 16-component spinor function of \(\sigma\)); as a four-dimensional
  field, \(\chi\) denotes the particle of a bound state. Unit:
  GeV\(^{2}\) (with the \(V_*\) normalisation). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \({}'\): The derivative of a function of the time \(x_4\) alone with
  respect to \(x_4\) (\(a_4' = \mathrm{d}a_4/\mathrm{d}x_4\); two primes
  the second derivative); on a function of \(\sigma\) alone (a profile)
  the derivative with respect to \(\sigma\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathbb{K}\): The transfer matrix of the profile equation at zero
  momentum, \(\chi' = \mathbb{K}\chi\). Unit: GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\gamma^M,\ \gamma^{(a)}\): The Dirac matrices of dirac16complex
  along the coordinates, \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^MT^a\),
  \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\); \(\gamma^{(a)} = T^a\) along the frame
  direction \((a)\) (\(\gamma^{(0)}\) along \(\sigma\), \(\gamma^{(4)}\)
  along the time). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(m_\Psi\): The mass parameter of dirac16complex in the bulk. Unit:
  GeV. Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\omega\): A frequency: the energy of a mode
  \(\propto \mathrm{e}^{-\mathrm{i}\omega x_4}\) (\(\omega_o\) of the
  orbital \(o\)). Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathbf{1},\ \mathbf{1}_8\): The identity matrix of the size fixed
  by the context (\(16 \times 16\) on dirac16complex, \(4 \times 4\) on
  a four-dimensional spinor, \(3 \times 3\) in generation or flavour
  space); \(\mathbf{1}_8\) is the \(8 \times 8\) identity matrix. Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(q\): The decay rate of a profile along \(\sigma\) at frequency
  \(\omega\), \(q = \sqrt{m_\Psi^2 - \omega^2}\) (imaginary in the
  continuum). Unit: GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\sigma\): The proper coordinate of the hidden direction:
  \(\mathrm{d}\sigma = \lvert\cot z\rvert\,\mathrm{d}x_0\),
  \(\sigma = \mathrm{sgn}(\pi/2 - z)\ln(\sin z)/(6H)\) on one period
  \(0 < z < \pi\); \(\sigma = 0\) is the fold \(z = \pi/2\),
  \(\sigma \to \mp\infty\) the tips \(z \to 0, \pi\); one period of
  \(x_0\) is the whole line \(-\infty < \sigma < \infty\). Unit: length.
  Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

At zero momentum along ordinary space (\(\vec k = 0\)) the profile
equation has constant coefficients on each side of the fold: \(\chi\) is
a combination of \(\mathrm{e}^{+q\sigma}\) and \(\mathrm{e}^{-q\sigma}\)
(eight solutions each). For \(\lvert\omega\rvert \ge m_\Psi\), \(q\) is
imaginary and the modes are waves along \(\sigma\): the bulk continuum,
which starts at the bulk mass \(m_\Psi\). For
\(\lvert\omega\rvert < m_\Psi\) the solutions grow or decay
exponentially, and only the fold terms (FE-39) can join decaying
solutions on the two sides into a normalisable state. With
chirality-dependent masses
\(m_{\mathrm{L}}P_{\mathrm{L}} + m_{\mathrm{R}}P_{\mathrm{R}}\) in place
of \(m_\Psi\) the threshold would be
\(\sqrt{m_{\mathrm{L}}m_{\mathrm{R}}}\) (tachyonic for
\(m_{\mathrm{L}}m_{\mathrm{R}} < 0\)); UM-3 keeps the record's single
bulk mass and puts the difference of the halves into the fold terms
(DERIVED, \texttt{um3\_spectra.py}, S1, and \texttt{um3\_bulk.py}, B4,
with the author's matrices). Status: DERIVED.

\[
\omega^2 = m_\Psi^2 + p_\sigma^2 + \mathrm{e}^{2H\lvert\sigma\rvert - 2a_4}\,\vec k^2 - \mathrm{e}^{2H\lvert\sigma\rvert + 2a_4}\,b_4^{-2}\,\vec\kappa^2 \tag{FE-38}
\] \textbf{Symbols in (FE-38).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\omega\): A frequency: the energy of a mode
  \(\propto \mathrm{e}^{-\mathrm{i}\omega x_4}\) (\(\omega_o\) of the
  orbital \(o\)). Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(m_\Psi\): The mass parameter of dirac16complex in the bulk. Unit:
  GeV. Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(p_\sigma\): The momentum along the hidden direction (local wave
  number of a profile along \(\sigma\)). Unit: GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(H\): The author's constant (an inverse length) that fixes the period
  \(\pi/(6H)\) of \(x_0\), the Lovelock couplings
  \(\alpha_{(p)} = w_{(p)}H^{2-2p}\) and the cosmological constant
  \(\Lambda = -7H^2\) (the author's original value \(-10H^2\) was
  corrected on 2026-10-25); in UM-3 a free parameter. Unit: GeV
  (1/length). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\sigma\): The proper coordinate of the hidden direction:
  \(\mathrm{d}\sigma = \lvert\cot z\rvert\,\mathrm{d}x_0\),
  \(\sigma = \mathrm{sgn}(\pi/2 - z)\ln(\sin z)/(6H)\) on one period
  \(0 < z < \pi\); \(\sigma = 0\) is the fold \(z = \pi/2\),
  \(\sigma \to \mp\infty\) the tips \(z \to 0, \pi\); one period of
  \(x_0\) is the whole line \(-\infty < \sigma < \infty\). Unit: length.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(a_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) that scales
  ordinary 3-space: on the fold \(\mathrm{e}^{a_4}\) is the scale factor
  of cosmology and \(a_4'\) the Hubble rate. Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\vec k\): The (comoving) momentum along ordinary space, \(\vec k^2\)
  its square. Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(b_4\): The author's metric function of the time \(x_4\) in the
  extra-time components, \(b_4 > 0\), never identically 1 (the author's
  order of 2026-10-24); the scale factor (the factor of the lengths) of
  the extra times is \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4\) on the fold and
  \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4\,\mathrm{e}^{-H\lvert\sigma\rvert}\) at the
  distance \(\sigma\) from it (the square root of the warp factor times
  \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4\)). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\vec\kappa\): A momentum along the extra times (three components);
  the fields of UM-3 have \(\vec\kappa = 0\). Unit: GeV. Status:
  DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

The local dispersion relation of dirac16complex (WKB: for profiles that
vary slowly compared with the warp), with the momentum \(p_\sigma\)
along the hidden direction and a momentum \(\vec\kappa\) along the extra
times. A momentum along a time-like direction enters with the negative
sign: modes that depend on the extra times have \(\omega^2 < 0\) for
large \(\lvert\vec\kappa\rvert\), they grow exponentially in \(x_4\)
(tachyons). The same holds for every field (the sign comes from
\(g^{rr} < 0\)). UM-3 therefore keeps only the fields that do not depend
on the extra times (ASSUMED); making the extra times small does not
help, because it only raises the lowest non-zero
\(\lvert\vec\kappa\rvert\). The restriction is consistent but not stable
(DERIVED, \texttt{um3\_extratimes.py}, EX1 to EX5): the metric does not
depend on \(x_5, x_6, x_7\), so the momentum along the extra times is
conserved and a field without it stays without it classically, but
interactions create pairs of opposite momenta (an ESTIMATE: no rate is
computed), and every mode of every field with a large enough momentum
\(\vec\kappa\) along the extra times is a tachyon. A state of
dirac16complex bound to the fold (FE-40, at the energy
\(\omega_{\mathrm{b}}\)) with the momentum \(\vec\kappa\) has
\(\omega^2 = \omega_{\mathrm{b}}^2 - c_{\mathrm{b}}^2\vec\kappa^2\),
where \(c_{\mathrm{b}}\) is the speed coefficient of the bound particles
along ordinary space
(\(\omega^2 = \omega_{\mathrm{b}}^2 + c_{\mathrm{b}}^2\vec k^2\) at
small momentum \(\vec k\) along ordinary space)
(\(c_{\mathrm{b}}^2 = 1.19\) for \(H = 0.05\,m_\Psi\) and the inverse
width \(q_{\mathrm{b}} = 0.3\,m_\Psi\)), and is no longer strictly
bound, because the warping multiplies its momentum along the extra times
by \(\mathrm{e}^{H\lvert\sigma\rvert}\) and raises it above the local
gap far from the fold (it leaks into the bulk at a rate of the order of
\(\mathrm{e}^{-2q_{\mathrm{b}}\sigma_{\mathrm{leak}}}\), an ESTIMATE);
on the frozen branch the tachyonic modes grow exponentially at a
constant rate (in the author's vacuum of the original weights they grew
double-exponentially); and compact extra times of the period
\(L_{\mathrm{t}}\) do not help: the first harmonic stays below one
e-folding over the age of the universe only for
\(L_{\mathrm{t}} \ge 26.6\) Gpc (\(2\pi\) times the age), but the
\(n\)-th harmonic grows \(n\) times faster, so infinitely many modes
grow for every \(L_{\mathrm{t}}\) (and a compact time-like direction is
itself a closed time-like curve). UM-3 needs a gauge principle that
removes the momentum along the extra times of every field. Status:
DERIVED (the dispersion, obtained by applying the Dirac operator of
FE-36 a second time, which turns the Dirac equation into a second-order
wave equation for every component, and the instability), ASSUMED (the
restriction), OPEN (the gauge principle).

\hypertarget{the-fold-and-the-states-bound-to-it}{%
\subsection{The fold and the states bound to
it}\label{the-fold-and-the-states-bound-to-it}}

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\chi(0^+) = \exp\Big(\gamma^{(0)}\big(\mu_{\mathrm{L}}P_{\mathrm{L}} + \mu_{\mathrm{R}}P_{\mathrm{R}}\big)\Big)\,\chi(0^-),\\
&\mu_X = \frac{1}{V_*}\Big[c_\Phi\,\langle\Phi^\dagger\Phi\rangle\,\delta_{X\mathrm{L}} + \frac14\big(c^{G}_X\langle G^2\rangle
 + c^{W}_X\langle W^2\rangle + c^{B}_X\langle B^2\rangle\big) + 2\omega\sum_f c^{f}_X\,n_f\Big],\qquad
 \langle\Phi^\dagger\Phi\rangle = \frac{v^2 + \mathcal{A}^2}{2}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-39}
\] \textbf{Symbols in (FE-39).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\chi(\sigma)\): The profile of a mode of dirac16complex along the
  hidden direction,
  \(\Psi = \mathrm{e}^{3H\lvert\sigma\rvert}\chi(\sigma)\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}(\vec k\cdot\vec x - \omega x_4)}\)
  (a 16-component spinor function of \(\sigma\)); as a four-dimensional
  field, \(\chi\) denotes the particle of a bound state. Unit:
  GeV\(^{2}\) (with the \(V_*\) normalisation). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\gamma^M,\ \gamma^{(a)}\): The Dirac matrices of dirac16complex
  along the coordinates, \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^MT^a\),
  \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\); \(\gamma^{(a)} = T^a\) along the frame
  direction \((a)\) (\(\gamma^{(0)}\) along \(\sigma\), \(\gamma^{(4)}\)
  along the time). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\mu_X,\ \mu_{\mathrm{L}},\ \mu_{\mathrm{R}}\): The dimensionless
  strengths of the fold for the left-handed and right-handed halves of
  dirac16complex, set by the mean fields of the Standard Model at the
  fold: the jump of the profile across the fold is the exponential of
  \(\gamma^{(0)}(\mu_{\mathrm{L}}P_{\mathrm{L}} + \mu_{\mathrm{R}}P_{\mathrm{R}})\);
  negative values attract. Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(P_{\mathrm{L}}\): The author's left-handed chiral projector,
  \(P_{\mathrm{L}} = (\mathbf{1} - \Gamma_8)/2\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(P_{\mathrm{R}}\): The author's right-handed chiral projector,
  \(P_{\mathrm{R}} = (\mathbf{1} + \Gamma_8)/2\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(X\): The label of a half of dirac16complex:
  \(X \in \{\mathrm{L}, \mathrm{R}\}\) (left-handed or right-handed
  half); a sum over \(X\) runs over both. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(V_*\): The physical volume of the extra times \(x_5, x_6, x_7\) on
  the fold in the late universe,
  \(V_* = V_{\mathrm{t}}(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4)^3\) (with
  \(V_{\mathrm{t}} = \int\mathrm{d}x_5\,\mathrm{d}x_6\,\mathrm{d}x_7\)
  the coordinate volume of the extra times) at a time when
  \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4\) is constant; it converts the
  eight-dimensional normalisation into the four-dimensional one. Unit:
  length\(^3\) (GeV\(^{-3}\)). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(c_\Phi\): The Higgs-portal coupling of the left-handed half of
  dirac16complex (the right-handed half has none). Unit: GeV\(^{-5}\).
  Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\Phi\): The Higgs doublet (two complex components, hypercharge
  \(1/2\)), a field of the fold. Unit: GeV. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \({}^\dagger\): Hermitian conjugation: the complex conjugate of the
  transpose of a matrix or spinor; for an operator on the space of
  states, its adjoint with respect to the scalar product of that space
  (for the quantised dirac16complex the indefinite Krein scalar
  product). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\langle\cdots\rangle\): The mean (expectation) value of an operator
  in the state of the medium (the vacuum, a plasma, the Kohn-Sham
  state). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\delta\): The Kronecker delta: with two indices \(\delta^M_N = 1\)
  if \(M = N\) and 0 otherwise (likewise \(\delta_{X\mathrm{L}}\) is 1
  if the label \(X\) of a half of dirac16complex is L and 0 otherwise);
  with upper and lower index strings the generalised Kronecker delta
  \(\delta^{M_1\cdots M_n}_{N_1\cdots N_n}\), the determinant of the
  \(n \times n\) matrix of the Kronecker deltas \(\delta^{M_a}_{N_b}\)
  (\(+1\) or \(-1\) if the upper indices are an even or odd permutation
  of the lower ones, 0 otherwise). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(c^{G}_X\): The gluon-kinetic portal coupling of the half \(X\) of
  dirac16complex. Unit: GeV\(^{-7}\). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\langle G^2\rangle\): The mean square of the gluon field strength,
  \(\langle G^A_{\mu\nu}G^{A\mu\nu}\rangle\) (the gluon condensate in
  the QCD vacuum, a thermal value in the plasma). Unit: GeV\(^4\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(G^A_\mu,\ G^A_{\mu\nu}\): The gluon field of \(SU(3)_C\) on the fold
  (\(A = 1, \dots, 8\)) and its field strength. Unit: GeV (field),
  GeV\(^2\) (field strength). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(c^{W}_X\): The \(W\)-kinetic portal coupling of the half \(X\).
  Unit: GeV\(^{-7}\). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\langle W^2\rangle\): The mean square of the \(SU(2)\) field
  strength, \(\langle W^I_{\mu\nu}W^{I\mu\nu}\rangle\). Unit: GeV\(^4\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(W^I_\mu,\ W^I_{\mu\nu}\): The gauge field of \(SU(2)_L\) on the
  fold, \(W^1_\mu, W^2_\mu, W^3_\mu\), and its field strength. Unit: GeV
  (field), GeV\(^2\) (field strength). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(c^{B}_X\): The \(B\)-kinetic portal coupling of the half \(X\).
  Unit: GeV\(^{-7}\). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(\langle B^2\rangle\): The mean square of the hypercharge field
  strength, \(\langle B_{\mu\nu}B^{\mu\nu}\rangle\). Unit: GeV\(^4\).
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(B_\mu,\ B_{\mu\nu}\): The hypercharge gauge field of \(U(1)_Y\) on
  the fold and its field strength. Unit: GeV (field), GeV\(^2\) (field
  strength). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\omega\): A frequency: the energy of a mode
  \(\propto \mathrm{e}^{-\mathrm{i}\omega x_4}\) (\(\omega_o\) of the
  orbital \(o\)). Unit: GeV. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(f\): The label of a kind of Standard-Model multiplet:
  \(f \in \{Q, u, d, \ell, e, \nu\}\) (quark doublets, up-quark
  singlets, down-quark singlets, lepton doublets, charged-lepton
  singlets, right-handed neutrinos); a sum over \(f\) runs over the six.
  Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(c^{f}_X\): The current-portal coupling of the half \(X\) of
  dirac16complex with the fermion-number current of the multiplets of
  kind \(f\) (flavour blind); \(c^{\ell}_X\) and \(c^{\nu}_X\) are those
  of the lepton doublets and of the right-handed neutrinos. Unit:
  GeV\(^{-7}\). Status: INVENTED.
\item
  \(n_f\): The number density of the Standard-Model fermions of kind
  \(f\) (particles minus antiparticles, summed over generations and
  gauge components), the time component of the mean current
  \(\langle J_f^\mu\rangle\) in the rest frame of the medium. Unit:
  GeV\(^3\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(v\): The vacuum expectation value of the lower component of the
  Higgs doublet, \(v^2 = -\mu^2/\lambda\). Unit: GeV. Value: 246.22 GeV
  \(= (\sqrt2\,G_{\mathrm{F}})^{-1/2}\) with the Fermi constant
  \(G_{\mathrm{F}} = 1.1663788\times10^{-5}\) GeV\(^{-2}\) of the muon
  lifetime (Particle Data Group). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{A}\): The author's ad hoc upper component of the Higgs
  vacuum, almightyAdHoc,
  \(\mathcal{A}(x_4) = v_{\mathcal{A}}\mathrm{e}^{-2a_{40}x_4}\). Unit:
  GeV. Status: AUTHOR.
\end{itemize}

The matching condition FE-13 for a mode, with the Standard-Model fields
replaced by their mean values in a homogeneous, isotropic medium at rest
on the fold (no mean field strength, no mean spatial current): the fold
acts on dirac16complex as a chirality-dependent mass concentrated at
\(\sigma = 0\), with the dimensionless strengths \(\mu_{\mathrm{L}}\)
and \(\mu_{\mathrm{R}}\). The Higgs portal acts on the left-handed half
only (\(\delta_{X\mathrm{L}} = 1\) for \(X = \mathrm{L}\), 0 for
\(X = \mathrm{R}\)); the gauge-kinetic portals act through the mean
squares \(\langle G^2\rangle = \langle G^A_{\mu\nu}G^{A\mu\nu}\rangle\)
(the gluon condensate of the QCD vacuum, or of the plasma),
\(\langle W^2\rangle\), \(\langle B^2\rangle\); the current portals act
through the number densities \(n_f\) of the Standard-Model fermions of
kind \(f\) (they give an energy-dependent strength). In the vacuum of
the late universe only the Higgs term and the gluon condensate
contribute.
\((\gamma^{(0)}(\mu_{\mathrm{L}}P_{\mathrm{L}} + \mu_{\mathrm{R}}P_{\mathrm{R}}))^2 = \mu_{\mathrm{L}}\mu_{\mathrm{R}}\mathbf{1}\),
so the exponential is
\(\cosh\varsigma + (\sinh\varsigma/\varsigma)\gamma^{(0)}(\mu_{\mathrm{L}}P_{\mathrm{L}} + \mu_{\mathrm{R}}P_{\mathrm{R}})\)
with \(\varsigma^2 = \mu_{\mathrm{L}}\mu_{\mathrm{R}}\); for
\(\mu_{\mathrm{R}} = 0\) it is exactly
\(1 + \mu_{\mathrm{L}}\gamma^{(0)}P_{\mathrm{L}}\) (DERIVED,
\texttt{um3\_spectra.py}, S2). Status: DERIVED.

\[
q_{\mathrm{b}} = -m_\Psi\,\big(\mu_{\mathrm{L}} + \mu_{\mathrm{R}}\big)\,\frac{\tanh\varsigma}{2\varsigma},\qquad \varsigma = \sqrt{\mu_{\mathrm{L}}\mu_{\mathrm{R}}},\qquad
\omega_{\mathrm{b}} = \sqrt{m_\Psi^2 - q_{\mathrm{b}}^2},\qquad \lvert\chi_{\mathrm{b}}(\sigma)\rvert \propto \mathrm{e}^{-q_{\mathrm{b}}\lvert\sigma\rvert} \tag{FE-40}
\] \textbf{Symbols in (FE-40).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(q_{\mathrm{b}}\): The inverse width of a state of dirac16complex
  bound to the fold: its profile falls as
  \(\mathrm{e}^{-q_{\mathrm{b}}\lvert\sigma\rvert}\). Unit: GeV. Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(m_\Psi\): The mass parameter of dirac16complex in the bulk. Unit:
  GeV. Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\mu_X,\ \mu_{\mathrm{L}},\ \mu_{\mathrm{R}}\): The dimensionless
  strengths of the fold for the left-handed and right-handed halves of
  dirac16complex, set by the mean fields of the Standard Model at the
  fold: the jump of the profile across the fold is the exponential of
  \(\gamma^{(0)}(\mu_{\mathrm{L}}P_{\mathrm{L}} + \mu_{\mathrm{R}}P_{\mathrm{R}})\);
  negative values attract. Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\varsigma\): The auxiliary variable of the bound-state condition,
  \(\varsigma = \sqrt{\mu_{\mathrm{L}}\mu_{\mathrm{R}}}\)
  (\(\varsigma_- = \sqrt{-\mu_{\mathrm{L}}\mu_{\mathrm{R}}}\) when
  \(\mu_{\mathrm{L}}\mu_{\mathrm{R}} < 0\)). Unit: dimensionless.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\omega_{\mathrm{b}}\): The energy of a state of dirac16complex bound
  to the fold at zero momentum: the four-dimensional mass of the
  particle \(\chi\). Unit: GeV. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\chi_{\mathrm{b}}\): The profile of a state of dirac16complex bound
  to the fold. Unit: GeV\(^{2}\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\sigma\): The proper coordinate of the hidden direction:
  \(\mathrm{d}\sigma = \lvert\cot z\rvert\,\mathrm{d}x_0\),
  \(\sigma = \mathrm{sgn}(\pi/2 - z)\ln(\sin z)/(6H)\) on one period
  \(0 < z < \pi\); \(\sigma = 0\) is the fold \(z = \pi/2\),
  \(\sigma \to \mp\infty\) the tips \(z \to 0, \pi\); one period of
  \(x_0\) is the whole line \(-\infty < \sigma < \infty\). Unit: length.
  Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

The states of dirac16complex bound to the fold at zero momentum: the
decaying solutions of FE-37 on the two sides, joined by FE-39. A bound
state exists if and only if \(0 < q_{\mathrm{b}} < m_\Psi\), that is if
the fold is attractive, \(\mu_{\mathrm{L}} + \mu_{\mathrm{R}} < 0\) (for
\(\mu_{\mathrm{L}}\mu_{\mathrm{R}} < 0\), \(\tanh(\varsigma)/\varsigma\)
means \(\tan(\varsigma_-)/\varsigma_-\) with
\(\varsigma_- = \sqrt{-\mu_{\mathrm{L}}\mu_{\mathrm{R}}}\)). Its energy
\(\omega_{\mathrm{b}}\) is the four-dimensional rest mass of a particle
that lives near the fold, with the profile
\(\mathrm{e}^{-q_{\mathrm{b}}\lvert\sigma\rvert}\) of width
\(1/q_{\mathrm{b}}\) along \(\sigma\): the ``\(x_0\) mass'' of
dirac16complex. The bound states are degenerate eight times at
\(+\omega_{\mathrm{b}}\) and eight times at \(-\omega_{\mathrm{b}}\)
(DERIVED, \texttt{um3\_spectra.py}, S3: the roots of the \(8 \times 8\)
matching determinant with the author's \(16 \times 16\) matrices agree
with this formula to \(10^{-7}\) in seven cases, with and without bound
states). For the Higgs portal alone (\(\mu_{\mathrm{R}} = 0\)):
\(q_{\mathrm{b}} = -m_\Psi\mu_{\mathrm{L}}/2\),
\(\omega_{\mathrm{b}} = m_\Psi\sqrt{1 - \mu_{\mathrm{L}}^2/4}\). Status:
DERIVED.

\hypertarget{the-kohn-sham-equations}{%
\subsection{The Kohn-Sham equations}\label{the-kohn-sham-equations}}

\[
\begin{aligned}
&\big[\gamma^MD_M - m_\Psi - \lambda_\Psi\,n_{\mathrm{s}} + \lambda_\Psi\,\mathcal{N}\big]\varphi_o
 = \delta_{\mathrm{D}}(\sigma)\,\frac{1}{V_*}\,\langle\mathbb{M}\rangle\,\varphi_o,\\
&\mathcal{N} = \sum_o\mathsf{n}_o\,\varphi_o\bar\varphi_o,\qquad n_{\mathrm{s}} = \mathrm{tr}\,\mathcal{N} = \sum_o\mathsf{n}_o\,\bar\varphi_o\varphi_o,\qquad
 \mathsf{n}_o = \frac{1}{\mathrm{e}^{(E_o - \mu_\Psi)/\mathsf{T}_\Psi} + 1}
\end{aligned} \tag{FE-41}
\] \textbf{Symbols in (FE-41).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\gamma^M,\ \gamma^{(a)}\): The Dirac matrices of dirac16complex
  along the coordinates, \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^MT^a\),
  \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\); \(\gamma^{(a)} = T^a\) along the frame
  direction \((a)\) (\(\gamma^{(0)}\) along \(\sigma\), \(\gamma^{(4)}\)
  along the time). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(D_M,\ D_\mu\): The covariant derivative: on dirac16complex
  \(D_M = \partial_M + \Omega_M\) (with
  \(D_M\bar\Psi = \partial_M\bar\Psi - \bar\Psi\Omega_M\)); on the
  fields of the fold the gauge and spin covariant derivative \(D_\mu\)
  (the partial derivative plus the spin connection of the fold and the
  gauge fields times their couplings and generators);
  \(D^\mu = \hat h^{\mu\nu}D_\nu\); on an adjoint field
  \((D_\mu X)^A = \partial_\mu X^A + g_3f^{ABC}G^B_\mu X^C\) (likewise
  for \(SU(2)\) with \(g_2\epsilon^{IJK}\)). Unit: 1/length. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(m_\Psi\): The mass parameter of dirac16complex in the bulk. Unit:
  GeV. Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\lambda_\Psi\): The coupling of the contact self-interaction of
  dirac16complex. Unit: GeV\(^{-6}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{s}}\): The scalar density of dirac16complex in the
  Kohn-Sham state,
  \(n_{\mathrm{s}} = \langle\bar\Psi\Psi\rangle = \mathrm{tr}\,\mathcal{N}\).
  Unit: GeV\(^7\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathcal{N}\): The one-body density matrix of dirac16complex,
  \(\mathcal{N} = \sum_o\mathsf{n}_o\varphi_o\bar\varphi_o\) (a
  \(16 \times 16\) matrix at each point). Unit: GeV\(^7\). Status:
  DERIVED.
\item
  \(\varphi_o,\ \bar\varphi_o\): A Kohn-Sham orbital of dirac16complex
  (a single-particle solution of FE-41) and its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  GeV\(^{7/2}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(o\): The label of a single-particle state of dirac16complex: a
  Kohn-Sham orbital, or a mode of the quantised field; a sum over \(o\)
  runs over the orbitals or the modes. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\delta_{\mathrm{D}}\): The Dirac delta function:
  \(\int\mathrm{d}\sigma\,\delta_{\mathrm{D}}(\sigma)F(\sigma) = F(0)\).
  Unit: 1/length (for \(\delta_{\mathrm{D}}(\sigma)\)). Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\sigma\): The proper coordinate of the hidden direction:
  \(\mathrm{d}\sigma = \lvert\cot z\rvert\,\mathrm{d}x_0\),
  \(\sigma = \mathrm{sgn}(\pi/2 - z)\ln(\sin z)/(6H)\) on one period
  \(0 < z < \pi\); \(\sigma = 0\) is the fold \(z = \pi/2\),
  \(\sigma \to \mp\infty\) the tips \(z \to 0, \pi\); one period of
  \(x_0\) is the whole line \(-\infty < \sigma < \infty\). Unit: length.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(V_*\): The physical volume of the extra times \(x_5, x_6, x_7\) on
  the fold in the late universe,
  \(V_* = V_{\mathrm{t}}(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4)^3\) (with
  \(V_{\mathrm{t}} = \int\mathrm{d}x_5\,\mathrm{d}x_6\,\mathrm{d}x_7\)
  the coordinate volume of the extra times) at a time when
  \(\mathrm{e}^{-a_4}b_4\) is constant; it converts the
  eight-dimensional normalisation into the four-dimensional one. Unit:
  length\(^3\) (GeV\(^{-3}\)). Status: ASSUMED.
\item
  \(\langle\cdots\rangle\): The mean (expectation) value of an operator
  in the state of the medium (the vacuum, a plasma, the Kohn-Sham
  state). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathbb{M}\): The fold operator of dirac16complex: the
  \(16 \times 16\) matrix (with a derivative along the fold) built from
  the Standard-Model fields at the fold by varying the portals with
  respect to \(\bar\Psi\) (FE-13). Unit: GeV\(^{-3}\) (so that
  \(\mathbb{M}/V_*\) is dimensionless). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathsf{n}_o\): The occupation of the Kohn-Sham orbital \(o\)
  (between 0 and 1). Unit: dimensionless. Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{tr}\): The trace of a matrix: the sum of its diagonal
  entries. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathrm{e}\): Euler's number, the base of the natural logarithm;
  \(\mathrm{e}^{X}\) is the exponential of \(X\). Unit: dimensionless.
  Value: 2.718281828. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(E\): An energy: \(E_o\) the energy of the Kohn-Sham orbital \(o\);
  in the neutrino sector the energy of a neutrino. Unit: GeV. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mu_\Psi\): The chemical potential of dirac16complex. Unit: GeV.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathsf{T}_\Psi\): The temperature of dirac16complex. Unit: GeV.
  Status: DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

The Kohn-Sham equations of dirac16complex coupled to itself, to gravity
and to every Standard-Model field: the quantised field is described by
single-particle orbitals \(\varphi_o\) with the occupations
\(\mathsf{n}_o\) (Fermi-Dirac with the temperature \(\mathsf{T}_\Psi\)
and the chemical potential \(\mu_\Psi\) of dirac16complex;
\(\mathsf{n}_o = 0\) or 1 in a degenerate state). Each orbital obeys the
field equation with the mean fields: gravity through \(D_M\) and
\(\gamma^M\) (the background metric and its spin connection), the
self-interaction through the Hartree term
\(\lambda_\Psi n_{\mathrm{s}}\) (the scalar density) and the exact
exchange term \(-\lambda_\Psi\mathcal{N}\) (the one-body density matrix,
a \(16 \times 16\) matrix at each point), and every Standard-Model field
through the fold operator with the mean fields of the Standard Model,
\(\langle\mathbb{M}\rangle\) (FE-13, FE-39). The mean-field
decomposition of \(-\frac{\lambda_\Psi}{2}(\bar\Psi\Psi)^2\) is
\(-\lambda_\Psi n_{\mathrm{s}}\bar\Psi\Psi + \lambda_\Psi\bar\Psi\mathcal{N}\Psi\)
(the second term with the sign of fermion exchange); correlation beyond
exchange is neglected and the negative-energy sea is not included
(no-sea approximation). Status: RECORD (the Kohn-Sham treatment),
DERIVED (the exchange term), ASSUMED (no correlation, no sea).

\[
\langle{\mathcal{T}_\Psi}_{MN}\rangle = -\frac14\sum_o\mathsf{n}_o\Big(\bar\varphi_o\gamma_MD_N\varphi_o + \bar\varphi_o\gamma_ND_M\varphi_o
 - (D_M\bar\varphi_o)\gamma_N\varphi_o - (D_N\bar\varphi_o)\gamma_M\varphi_o\Big) + \frac{\lambda_\Psi}{2}\,g_{MN}\big(n_{\mathrm{s}}^2 - \mathrm{tr}\,\mathcal{N}^2\big) \tag{FE-42}
\] \textbf{Symbols in (FE-42).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(\langle\cdots\rangle\): The mean (expectation) value of an operator
  in the state of the medium (the vacuum, a plasma, the Kohn-Sham
  state). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{T}_\Psi\): The energy-momentum tensor of dirac16complex,
  \(\mathcal{T}_{MN} = -(2/\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert})\,\delta S_\Psi/\delta g^{MN}\).
  Unit: GeV\(^8\). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(M\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also with a numbered
  copy \(M_1, M_2, \dots\)): it runs over \(x_0, x_1, \dots, x_7\); a
  repeated index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(N\): A coordinate index of eight dimensions (also
  \(N_1, N_2, \dots\)), running over \(x_0, \dots, x_7\); a repeated
  index is summed. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(o\): The label of a single-particle state of dirac16complex: a
  Kohn-Sham orbital, or a mode of the quantised field; a sum over \(o\)
  runs over the orbitals or the modes. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathsf{n}_o\): The occupation of the Kohn-Sham orbital \(o\)
  (between 0 and 1). Unit: dimensionless. Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\varphi_o,\ \bar\varphi_o\): A Kohn-Sham orbital of dirac16complex
  (a single-particle solution of FE-41) and its Dirac adjoint. Unit:
  GeV\(^{7/2}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(\gamma^M,\ \gamma^{(a)}\): The Dirac matrices of dirac16complex
  along the coordinates, \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^MT^a\),
  \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\); \(\gamma^{(a)} = T^a\) along the frame
  direction \((a)\) (\(\gamma^{(0)}\) along \(\sigma\), \(\gamma^{(4)}\)
  along the time). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(D_M,\ D_\mu\): The covariant derivative: on dirac16complex
  \(D_M = \partial_M + \Omega_M\) (with
  \(D_M\bar\Psi = \partial_M\bar\Psi - \bar\Psi\Omega_M\)); on the
  fields of the fold the gauge and spin covariant derivative \(D_\mu\)
  (the partial derivative plus the spin connection of the fold and the
  gauge fields times their couplings and generators);
  \(D^\mu = \hat h^{\mu\nu}D_\nu\); on an adjoint field
  \((D_\mu X)^A = \partial_\mu X^A + g_3f^{ABC}G^B_\mu X^C\) (likewise
  for \(SU(2)\) with \(g_2\epsilon^{IJK}\)). Unit: 1/length. Status:
  STANDARD.
\item
  \(\lambda_\Psi\): The coupling of the contact self-interaction of
  dirac16complex. Unit: GeV\(^{-6}\). Status: RECORD.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval,
  \(g^{MN}\) its inverse, \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its
  determinant (\(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper
  volume element). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(n_{\mathrm{s}}\): The scalar density of dirac16complex in the
  Kohn-Sham state,
  \(n_{\mathrm{s}} = \langle\bar\Psi\Psi\rangle = \mathrm{tr}\,\mathcal{N}\).
  Unit: GeV\(^7\). Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{tr}\): The trace of a matrix: the sum of its diagonal
  entries. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathcal{N}\): The one-body density matrix of dirac16complex,
  \(\mathcal{N} = \sum_o\mathsf{n}_o\varphi_o\bar\varphi_o\) (a
  \(16 \times 16\) matrix at each point). Unit: GeV\(^7\). Status:
  DERIVED.
\end{itemize}

The energy-momentum tensor of the Kohn-Sham state, the source of
dirac16complex in FE-1 and, through the portals, in FE-5: the sum over
the occupied orbitals of FE-6, and the mean value of the
self-interaction (on solutions of FE-41,
\(\langle\mathcal{L}_\Psi\rangle = \frac{\lambda_\Psi}{2}(n_{\mathrm{s}}^2 - \mathrm{tr}\,\mathcal{N}^2)\)).
It closes the loop: the orbitals determine the densities, the densities
the mean fields and the metric, the metric and the mean fields the
orbitals. Status: DERIVED.

\[
Q_\Psi = \int\mathrm{d}\sigma\,\mathrm{d}^3x\,\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\;\Psi^\dagger\mathfrak{B}\Psi,\qquad
\mathfrak{B} = \mathrm{i}\,\sigma_{16}\,\gamma^{(4)},\qquad \mathfrak{B}^\dagger = \mathfrak{B},\qquad \mathfrak{B}^2 = \mathbf{1},\qquad
E_o = -\omega_o\,\mathcal{Q}_o \tag{FE-43}
\] \textbf{Symbols in (FE-43).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \(Q_\Psi\): The conserved charge of dirac16complex (the Noether charge
  of a change of the phase of \(\Psi\) by a constant), a Krein form
  (indefinite); as written it is the charge per unit coordinate volume
  of the extra times, on which no field depends. Unit: GeV\(^3\).
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{d}\): The differential: \(\mathrm{d}X\) is an infinitesimal
  change of \(X\), \(\mathrm{d}X/\mathrm{d}Y\) the derivative of \(X\)
  with respect to \(Y\), \(\mathrm{d}^nX\) the volume element of \(n\)
  variables. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\sigma\): The proper coordinate of the hidden direction:
  \(\mathrm{d}\sigma = \lvert\cot z\rvert\,\mathrm{d}x_0\),
  \(\sigma = \mathrm{sgn}(\pi/2 - z)\ln(\sin z)/(6H)\) on one period
  \(0 < z < \pi\); \(\sigma = 0\) is the fold \(z = \pi/2\),
  \(\sigma \to \mp\infty\) the tips \(z \to 0, \pi\); one period of
  \(x_0\) is the whole line \(-\infty < \sigma < \infty\). Unit: length.
  Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(x\): The coordinates of the fold: \(x_i\) (\(i = 1, 2, 3\)) of
  ordinary space, \(x_4\) the time, \(x_r\) (\(r = 5, 6, 7\)) the extra
  times; a field of the fold is a function of the four observable
  coordinates \(x = (x_1, x_2, x_3, x_4)\), and
  \(\mathrm{d}^4x = \mathrm{d}x_1\,\mathrm{d}x_2\,\mathrm{d}x_3\,\mathrm{d}x_4\).
  Unit: length (each coordinate). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(g_{MN}\): The metric tensor of the eight-dimensional spacetime
  (signature \(+,+,+,+,-,-,-,-\) in the order \(x_0, \dots, x_7\)):
  \(g_{MN}\,\mathrm{d}X^M\mathrm{d}X^N\) is the squared interval,
  \(g^{MN}\) its inverse, \(\lvert g\rvert\) the absolute value of its
  determinant (\(\sqrt{\lvert g\rvert}\,\mathrm{d}^8X\) is the proper
  volume element). Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\Psi,\ \bar\Psi\): dirac16complex: the author's 16-component complex
  spinor field of eight dimensions;
  \(\bar\Psi = \Psi^\dagger\sigma_{16}\) is its Dirac adjoint; in a term
  of the fold, \(\Psi\) is its value at \(\sigma = 0\). Unit:
  GeV\(^{7/2}\). Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \({}^\dagger\): Hermitian conjugation: the complex conjugate of the
  transpose of a matrix or spinor; for an operator on the space of
  states, its adjoint with respect to the scalar product of that space
  (for the quantised dirac16complex the indefinite Krein scalar
  product). Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\mathfrak{B}\): The Krein operator,
  \(\mathfrak{B} = \mathrm{i}\sigma_{16}\gamma^{(4)}\): a Hermitian
  involution with the signature (8, 8); \(\Psi^\dagger\mathfrak{B}\Psi\)
  is the charge density. Unit: dimensionless. Status: DERIVED.
\item
  \(\mathrm{i}\): The imaginary unit, \(\mathrm{i}^2 = -1\). Unit:
  dimensionless. Status: STANDARD.
\item
  \(\sigma_{16}\): The matrix of the Dirac adjoint,
  \(\sigma_{16} = T_0T_1T_2T_3\), real and symmetric; \(\sigma_{16}T_a\)
  is real and antisymmetric. Unit: dimensionless. Status: AUTHOR.
\item
  \(\gamma^M,\ \gamma^{(a)}\): The Dirac matrices of dirac16complex
  along the coordinates, \(\gamma^M = \mathbf{e}_a{}^MT^a\),
  \(\gamma_M = g_{MN}\gamma^N\); \(\gamma^{(a)} = T^a\) along the frame
  direction \((a)\) (\(\gamma^{(0)}\) along \(\sigma\), \(\gamma^{(4)}\)
